Retrieval quality is therefore not the only bottleneck. Small bilingual legal models also differ in how they use supplied law and whether they answer in the requested language. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/momahadi/bangladesh-legal-qa-dataset.

</details>


### [216] [Two Regimes of Chain-of-Thought Unfaithfulness: Behavioral Detection Fails Where Models Are Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23458)
*Suramya R. Angdembay,Dikshant Aryal,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了思维链（CoT）解释的忠实性检测问题，发现答案正确性是区分忠实与不忠实CoT的关键结构变量：69%的不忠实出现在错误答案中，现有检测方法在正确答案区间表现中等，在错误答案区间完全失效，且标准指标与人类标注呈反相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT忠实性检测方法缺乏系统性评估，且忽视答案正确性这一核心结构维度，导致对不忠实CoT的检测存在盲区。

Method: 基于FaithCoT-Bench人工标注，对比多种黑箱检测方法（包括oracle诊断、行为信号、线性探针等），按答案正确性分层分析，并跨7模型验证。

Result: 答案错误oracle信号AUROC达0.696，优于所有专门信号；正确答案区间行为信号中等有效（0.63-0.67），错误答案区间所有信号均低于随机水平；标准删除步骤指标与人类标注反相关。

Conclusion: 答案正确性是CoT忠实性检测的核心结构变量，当前方法在错误答案区间完全失效，需重新设计检测范式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) explanations support oversight only if they are faithful: the stated reasoning must actually produce the answer. Auditing black-box (behavioral) detection of unfaithful CoT against FaithCoT-Bench's human annotations, we find answer correctness structures the problem at every level. Answer incorrectness alone (an oracle diagnostic, not a deployable detector) outperforms every purpose-built signal (AUROC 0.696), because 69% of annotated unfaithfulness occurs on incorrect answers. Stratifying by correctness splits detection into two regimes: on correct answers, behavioral signals moderately separate faithful from post-hoc reasoning (0.63-0.67); on incorrect answers, where most unfaithfulness lives, no tested signal is detectably above chance (replicated on all four models for benchmark-wide signals). The standard step-removal metric anti-correlates with human labels; this inversion reproduces on the benchmark's released scores and on hint-dependent counterfactually labeled traces. Linear probes decode the behaviorally blind regime in Llama-3.1-8B and the correct-answer regime in Qwen-2.5-7B, with no shared, positively aligned direction detected across regimes; instructed answer-first traces (7 models) transfer to neither annotated regime, while hint-induced unverbalized answer flips do, in model- and source-dependent settings. We also independently verify and resolve a documentation-data mismatch in the benchmark's label semantics.

</details>


### [217] [Mwando: Leveraging AI to Preserve and Teach shiKomori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23481)
*Naira Abdou Mohamed,Haidar Nassur Said Ali,Mohamed Hazra,Naoufal Mohamed Soibira,Roushnaty Ali Yamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mwando，一个面向科摩罗语shiKomori的虚拟教育助手，采用多智能体架构融合向量搜索、知识图谱和网络搜索回退机制，支持四种方言变体，在500个查询评估中表现出色，为低资源语言AI教育工具提供了蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: shiKomori作为科摩罗群岛语言属于低资源语言，缺乏计算化支持工具，面临消亡风险，亟需开发AI驱动的教育辅助系统来支持教学和语言保护。

Method: 构建包含短语、谚语、词典和语法课程的知识库，采用多智能体架构，结合向量搜索、知识图谱和网络搜索回退机制，实现准确且上下文感知的响应。

Result: 在500个查询上评估，在词汇查找和语法解释方面表现强劲；定性案例研究展示了系统能力与当前局限性。

Conclusion: Mwando是shiKomori计算化支持的初步尝试，为开发其他低资源语言的AI教育工具提供了可复用的蓝图。

Abstract: This paper presents Mwando, a virtual educational assistant designed to support the teaching and preservation of shiKomori, the language of the Comoros Islands. The system covers the four main dialectal variants (shiNgazidja, shiMwali, shiNdzuani and shiMaore) through a knowledge base constructed from phrases, proverbs, dictionaries and grammar lessons. A multi-agent architecture combining vector search, a knowledge graph and web search fallback enables accurate and context-aware responses. Evaluation on 500 queries demonstrates strong performance on vocabulary lookup and grammar explanations, while qualitative case studies illustrate both capabilities and current limitations. This work represents an initial step toward computational support for shiKomori and provides a blueprint for developing AI-powered educational tools for other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [218] [The Cross-Domain Generalization Cost of Offensive Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23512)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Xiaoke Sun,Qiuping Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套诊断与优化框架，将跨数据集和跨语言的性能下降分解为数据集效应和语言效应，并通过对比微调与联合训练策略，量化了源任务损伤与多语言能力提升之间的帕累托权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多仅报告跨数据集/跨语言部署时的性能下降现象，缺乏系统性的方法论来分解性能下降的成因并量化修复成本。

Method: 提出三部分组成的诊断与优化框架：(1) 零样本迁移损失分解，将性能下降分离为数据集效应和语言效应；(2) 受控微调协议，通过对比少样本学习曲线量化适应效率与源任务损伤；(3) 三种联合训练策略（结合温度采样和经验回放），在多语言能力与源任务性能间实现可控制的帕累托权衡。

Result: 数据集效应主导零样本迁移损失，远超语言效应；无回放机制的少样本适应虽数据高效，但造成的源任务损伤是联合训练策略的4到9倍且高度不稳定；三种联合训练策略以3.2-4.1个百分点的源任务性能损失换取8.1-42.6个百分点的多语言能力提升，形成清晰的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种系统化的方法来诊断和缓解跨数据集/跨语言的性能下降问题，通过可控的帕累托权衡策略，可在多语言能力提升与源任务性能保护之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Offensive language detection models generally suffer performance degradation when deployed across datasets and across languages, yet most existing studies stop at reporting this phenomenon and lack a systematic methodology for decomposing the causes of degradation into attributable components and quantifying the cost of remediation. This paper proposes a diagnosis and optimization framework composed of three coordinated technical components. First, a zero-shot transfer loss decomposition that separates the performance degradation from OLID to MLMA into two independently measurable components, namely dataset effect and language effect. Second, a controlled fine-tuning protocol that quantifies both adaptation efficiency and the hidden damage inflicted on the source task by comparing few shot learning curves under continued fine-tuning and cold-start starting points. Third, three joint training strategies incorpo rating temperature sampling and experience replay, which offer a controllable Pareto trade-off between improving multilingual capability and preserving source-task performance. Experiments built on this framework show that the dataset effect dominates the zero-shot transfer loss and substantially outweighs the language effect. Few-shot adaptation without a replay mechanism, though data-efficient, inflicts source task damage 4 to 9 times greater than that of the joint training strategies, and its damage magnitude is highly unstable. The three joint training strategies trade 3.2 to 4.1 percentage points of source-task performance for 8.1 to 42.6 percentage points of multilingual capability gain, forming a clear and controllable Pareto trade-off.

</details>


### [219] [Do Diagrams Help Large Language Models Reason? Evidence from Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23513)
*Risako Ando,Koji Mineshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种表示形式（自然语言、逻辑符号、线性图、欧拉图）对大型语言模型（Claude 3.5 Sonnet和GPT-4o-mini）在三段论推理中的影响，发现图示表示并不能持续提升模型性能，模型在蕴含和矛盾问题上表现良好，但在中性问题上存在系统性转换错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究表明欧拉图等图示表示能提升人类推理表现，但对其在大语言模型上的效果知之甚少，因此本文旨在探索不同表示形式对LLM逻辑推理能力的影响。

Method: 使用Ando等人(2024)的285个三段论推理问题，在四种表示条件（自然语言、逻辑符号、线性图、欧拉图）下，评估Claude 3.5 Sonnet和GPT-4o-mini两个当代LLM的推理表现。

Result: 图示表示未能持续提升模型性能；模型在蕴含和矛盾问题上表现良好，但在中性问题上表现较差，且存在系统性转换错误。

Conclusion: 当前测试的LLM在逻辑推理任务中从图示表示中获益有限。

Abstract: Diagrams are widely used to support logical reasoning, and prior studies suggest that representations such as Euler diagrams can improve human reasoning performance. Recent work has also explored their effects on large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we compare four representational conditions for syllogistic reasoning: natural language, logical notation, linear diagrams, and Euler diagrams. Using 285 problems from Ando et al. (2024), we evaluate two contemporary LLMs, Claude 3.5~Sonnet and GPT-4o-mini. Our results show that diagrammatic representations do not consistently improve performance. Although the models perform well on entailment and contradiction problems, they struggle with neutral problems and often make systematic conversion errors. Overall, the results suggest that the tested models gain limited benefit from diagrams in logical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [220] [Novel Claim or Déjà Vu? Rethinking "Contamination-Free'' Dynamic Evaluation for Multimodal Automated Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23514)
*Haorui He,Xinwen Chen,Dacheng Wen,Reynold Cheng,Francis C. M. Lau,Yupeng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态自动事实核查（MAFC）中的基准污染问题，发现即使是动态基准也无法完全消除污染风险，且污染会显著高估模型性能（Macro-F1最高提升11.34分），并扭曲系统排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准存在污染风险（过时claims可通过LLM内部知识验证），新兴动态基准虽假设无污染但缺乏实证检验。本文旨在系统研究两类基准的污染风险及其对MAFC评估的影响。

Method: 基于静态AVeriTeC基准和新构建的动态ClaimReview2025Q4基准，通过实验分析污染风险，并设计严格污染控制的评估设置对SOTA LLM进行重新评估。

Result: （1）动态评估可降低但无法消除污染风险，17.09%-29.30%的post-cut-off claims仍可能被污染；（2）许多新发布的claims可通过cut-off前的公开知识直接或组合验证；（3）污染可使Macro-F1虚高最多11.34分，并扭曲系统排名。

Conclusion: 提出可信MAFC评估的实践指南，强调必须进行严格的污染控制以确保评估结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal automated fact-checking (MAFC) verifies claims by retrieving and reasoning over external evidence. However, most existing static benchmarks risk contamination: they primarily consist of outdated claims verifiable using an LLM's internal knowledge without external evidence. This can inflate performance estimates and fail to reflect true capability on novel claims that require up-to-date information. To address this, emerging dynamic benchmarks collect claims published after LLMs' knowledge cut-off dates, assuming they are uncontaminated. This work revisits this assumption by empirically studying contamination risks in both the state-of-the-art (SOTA) static AVeriTeC benchmark and our newly constructed dynamic ClaimReview2025Q4 benchmark, as well as their impact on MAFC evaluation. Our experiments yield 16 findings, highlighting three key results: (1) Dynamic evaluation reduces but does not eliminate contamination risks, as 17.09\%--29.30\% of post-cut-off claims remain potentially contaminated; (2) Many newly published claims can be verified either directly or by synthesizing multiple pieces of public knowledge available before the cut-off; and (3) Contamination can induce statistically significant inflation in MAFC performance, increasing Macro-F1 by up to 11.34 points and distorting system rankings. In light of these findings, we re-evaluate SOTA LLMs under a strictly contamination-controlled setting. Our study provides practical guidelines for trustworthy MAFC evaluation.

</details>


### [221] [The JEPA Paradox in Language: The Geometry of Linguistic Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23531)
*Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了确定性JEPA风格潜在预测在文本编码中失效的根本原因：语言的条件结构允许多种合理补全，而平方误差潜在预测要求目标表示集中在单一有意义点，导致质心退化和坍缩压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管JEPA在图像、视频和音频中表现优异，但确定性JEPA式潜在预测并未成为文本编码器的标准目标。作者认为这一差距反映了平方误差潜在预测与语言条件结构之间的不匹配，亟需澄清其根本原因。

Method: 形式化了三个条件（可预测性、非坍缩、低条件方差），通过匹配的I-JEPA和T-JEPA实验验证了失败序列：互信息饱和与目标方差升高→训练-验证不稳定→有效秩退化→余弦坍缩→下游迁移差。使用了五个独立数据种子验证非采样伪影。

Result: 文本中确定性JEPA训练表现出互信息饱和、目标方差升高、有效秩退化、余弦坍缩和下游迁移性能下降的连锁失败模式，且该模式在五个数据种子中一致复现。

Conclusion: 结果并不否定语言中的预测学习，但表明文本兼容的JEPA目标必须保留多种可能的补全，而非将其压缩到单个潜在点。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are effective for images, video, and audio, yet deterministic JEPA-style latent prediction has not become a standard objective for text encoders. We argue that this gap reflects a mismatch between squared-error latent prediction and the conditional structure of language. The key requirement is conditional concentration: given a context and target location, the target representation should lie near a single meaningful point. Local image prediction often satisfies this through spatial continuity, whereas masked text can admit multiple valid token or span completions whose representations need not share a coherent center. We formalize this mismatch through three conditions---predictability, non-collapse, and low conditional variance---and show how their failure creates centroid degeneracy and collapse pressure in text. Matched I-JEPA and T-JEPA experiments reveal the predicted sequence: mutual-information saturation and elevated target variance precede train--validation instability, effective-rank degeneration, cosine collapse, and poor downstream transfer. The same pattern appears across five independent data seeds, indicating that it is not a sampling artifact. These results do not rule out predictive learning for language; they show that text-compatible JEPA objectives must preserve multiple plausible completions rather than compress them into a single latent point.

</details>


### [222] [Guiding Language Models to Be More Empathetic: Culturally Sensitive Mental Health Advice Generation Through Human-LLM Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23538)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,Md. Mahfuzur Rahman,Khan Md Hasib,Jubayer Al Mahmud,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对低资源语言中的共情心理健康咨询问题，构建了625个真实心理案例的评估语料库，提出RP-RCAF提示策略和G-REFS评估框架，实验表明该方法在情感敏感性、文化适切性等维度上优于传统提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在低资源语言环境下生成共情心理健康咨询回复的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从Facebook帖子、电视节目和学生问卷三方面收集625个真实案例，构建由临床心理学家撰写的建议和三种LLM生成回复组成的评估语料库；提出RP-RCAF提示策略（结合少样本示例与结构化自我反思）和G-REFS评估框架（自动化评估+专家验证）。

Result: RP-RCAF在所有评估模型上均优于传统提示方法，生成的回复更接近专业心理咨询标准。

Conclusion: 提出的RP-RCAF策略能有效提升LLM在低资源语言下的心理健康咨询回复质量，尤其在文化适切性和伦理对齐方面表现突出。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), their ability to generate empathetic mental health counseling responses in low-resource languages remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we curate 625 authentic mental health cases from three complementary sources: (1) publicly available Facebook posts discussing mental health concerns, (2) transcripts from the Bangladeshi television program "Ami Akhon Ki Korbo", and (3) anonymized student questionnaire responses covering diverse emotional and psychological challenges. Based on these cases, we build an evaluation corpus comprising advice written by licensed clinical psychologists and responses generated by three modern proprietary LLMs: GPT-4o Mini, Claude 4.5 Haiku, and Gemini 2.5 Pro. We further propose the Role-Playing Reflective Chain-of-Thought Advisory Framework (RP-RCAF), a task-specific prompting strategy that combines expert-authored few-shot examples with structured self-reflection to produce supportive, culturally aware, and ethically aligned counseling through a compassionate advisor persona. We also introduce the Grok 4-Based Response Evaluation and Scoring Framework (G-REFS), which integrates automated assessment with expert psychologist validation across emotional sensitivity, cultural appropriateness, linguistic clarity, and ethical soundness. Experimental results show that RP-RCAF consistently outperforms conventional prompting across all evaluated models and produces responses that more closely align with professional psychological counseling.

</details>


### [223] [Language Shapes Instruction Hierarchy Compliance in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23545)
*Jiwon Moon,Yerin Hwang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出XIH-Bench基准，评估多语言环境下指令层级（IH）遵循情况，发现语言依赖不对称性和语言边界效应，揭示多语言可靠性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IH评估几乎仅限英语，多语言场景下模型是否稳定遵循IH尚不明确。

Method: 构建XIH-Bench基准，涵盖6种语言、4个领域、3种IH设置，包含同语言和跨语言冲突场景。

Result: 发现两种一致模式：(1) 语言依赖不对称性——同一语言在高优先级位置增强遵循，在低优先级位置则会破坏遵循；(2) 语言边界效应——跨语言冲突比同语言冲突的遵循率更高。

Conclusion: 语言专门化会使模型偏好语言中的低优先级指令更难被覆盖，带来多语言可靠性和安全风险。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy (IH) requires models to prioritize instructions by source, ensuring that higher-priority instructions override lower-priority ones. Despite its importance for safe and controllable deployment, existing evaluations have focused almost exclusively on English, leaving it unclear whether IH compliance remains stable in multilingual settings. We introduce XIH-Bench, a benchmark for multilingual IH evaluation with both same-language and cross-language conflicts across six languages, four domains, and three IH settings. Across models, we find two consistent patterns. First, IH compliance exhibits a clear language-dependent asymmetry: a language that strengthens compliance in the higher-priority position can become disruptive in the lower-priority position. Second, cross-language conflicts yield higher compliance than same-language conflicts, a phenomenon we term the Language Boundary Effect. We further show that language specialization can make lower-priority instructions in model-favored languages harder to override, creating multilingual reliability and security risks.

</details>


### [224] [GEMCo: A Validated, Ethically Releasable Proxy for Inaccessible Counselling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23621)
*Philipp Steigerwald,Eric Rudolph,Mara Stieler,Jennifer Burghardt,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEMCo是一个可公开的、由人类撰写的心理咨询代理数据集，包含86封完整的德语邮件咨询对话，经过验证与真实咨询数据差异小，可作为隐私受限领域的替代研究资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实心理咨询数据因隐私和伦理问题无法公开，限制了该领域的语言研究。作者希望构建一个可发布的、无隐私问题的代理数据集，以推动相关研究。

Method: 构建GEMCo（86封德语邮件咨询对话），由专家撰写案例并与训练有素的角色扮演者进行咨询；与124个真实咨询对话进行对比验证，分析咨询策略和客户情绪差异。

Result: 代理数据与真实数据在咨询策略和客户情绪上存在可检测但微小的差异；生成验证支持分析结果。

Conclusion: GEMCo可作为隐私受限心理咨询领域的可公开研究数据，且其验证方法可推广到任何真实数据无法共享但可构建人工代理的领域。

Abstract: This paper presents GEMCo, a releasable, human-written proxy for inaccessible counselling data: 86 complete German e-mail counselling conversations (728 messages), expert-authored cases and counsellor sessions with trained role-players. It is validated against a held-out reference of 124 real counselling conversations. The proxy and the real conversations are measured against each other in counsellor strategies and client emotions. The gap is detectable but small. A generative validation supports the analysis. The validation method itself generalises to any domain where real data cannot be shared but a human-made proxy can. Privacy and ethics keep real counselling data closed. GEMCo carries none by design and can be released -- a first step toward language research in this domain.

</details>


### [225] [EmoTrace: An Emotion Trajectory-Centered Framework for Psychological Support Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23648)
*Kaitong Weng,Lixin Liu,Zihao Liu,Bo Wang,Shiguang Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EmoTrace框架，通过建模求助者情绪轨迹生成多轮心理咨询对话语料，显著提升了对话的情绪丰富度和共情质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话数据生成方法存在求助者情绪稳定、情绪动态变化有限、过度顺从咨询师引导等问题，且咨询师回复过度聚焦问题解决而忽视情绪导向互动，导致LLM无法有效应对情绪不稳定场景。

Method: 构建EmoTrace框架，包含求助者认知画像、带情绪图式与激活机制的求助者模块、咨询师模块以及情绪轨迹控制模块，增强求助者情绪表达的层次性和咨询师针对性共情表达。

Result: 实验结果表明EmoTrace在情绪丰富度和共情质量上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过建模情绪轨迹可有效提升心理咨询对话语料质量，增强LLM应对情绪不稳定场景的能力。

Abstract: Using large language models (LLMs) to assist psychological counseling is an important task in the field of natural language processing. The construction of high-quality psychological support dialogue corpora serves as a critical foundation for training counseling-oriented conversational models. However, existing data generation approaches generally suffer from several limitations, including emotionally stable seekers, limited variation in emotional dynamics, and a high degree of compliance with counselors' guidance. These issues result in LLM that lack the capability to effectively respond to emotionally unstable scenarios. In addition, counselor responses are typically driven by problem-solving objectives, thereby overlooking the role of emotion-focused interaction, which are essential in psychological counseling. To address these gaps, we propose EmoTrace, a multi-turn dialogue corpus generation framework centered on modeling seekers' emotional trajectories. we construct seekers' cognitive profile and introduce a seeker module with emotional schemas and an associated activation mechanism, a counselor module, and an emotional trajectory control module, thereby enhancing the layering of the seeker's emotional expression and the counselor's targeted empathic expression. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing approaches in terms of emotional richness and empathy quality.

</details>


### [226] [An empirical investigation into the properties of standard word embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23675)
*Salomon Kabongo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了词嵌入（将词序列映射到连续向量空间）的各种计算方法，调研了公开可用的工具包和嵌入矩阵，并通过实验分析了几种实现的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词嵌入是自然语言处理领域近年最重要的发展之一，在语音识别、机器翻译、情感分析等多个任务中具有广泛应用，因此有必要系统性回顾其计算机制并进行实验分析。

Method: 综述了多种词嵌入计算机制，调研了公开工具包和嵌入矩阵，并选取一种或多种实现进行实验以理解其特征。

Result: 对词嵌入的不同计算方法进行了梳理和分类，总结了现有工具包和嵌入矩阵的可用情况，并通过实验揭示了所选实现的具体特性。

Conclusion: 词嵌入技术具有重要应用价值，不同计算机制各有特点，通过实验分析有助于更好地理解和选用合适的词嵌入方法。

Abstract: The embedding of word sequences into continuous vector spaces has been one of the most important developments in Natural Language Processing in the recent past. Such embeddings have found application in areas such as Automatic Speech Recognition, Machine Translation, Sentiment Analysis and many more. This essay reviews the various mechanisms that have been proposed for the calculation of word embeddings, investigates popular toolkits and embedding matrices that are available in the public domain, and experiments with one or more selected implementations to better understand their characteristics.
  La représentation vectorielle continue de mots a été l'un des développements les plus importants dans le domaine du traitement automatique du langage naturel au cours des dernières années. Ces représentations ont trouvé application dans des domaines tels que la reconnaissance vocale, la traduction automatique, l'analyse des sentiments, etc. Ce travail passe en revue les différents mécanismes proposés pour le calcul de ces vecteurs de mots, étudie les kits d'outils populaires et les matrices disponibles publiquement en ligne, et expérimente avec une ou plusieurs implémentations sélectionnées pour mieux comprendre leurs caractéristiques.

</details>


### [227] [Formally Verified Synthesizable Floating-Point Data Types in ARCH HDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23715)
*Shuqing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在硬件描述语言ARCH中实现IEEE-754 FP32和BF16算术的形式化验证方法，通过单一源码生成可综合SystemVerilog、SMT-LIB模型和Lean 4证明模型，并利用Yosys-to-SMT验证和Lean证明确保所有操作符（包括乘法和FMA）的正确性，其中FMA通过缩小精度位宽实现可流水线化并验证等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件算术单元的形式化验证面临两个挑战：一是不同验证工件（RTL实现、SMT模型、交互式证明）之间容易漂移不一致；二是乘法和FMA等操作由于SAT求解困难而无法穷举验证。作者希望设计一种从单一源码生成多个验证工件的方法，在保证各工件结构一致的同时，解决乘法器相关操作的验证难题。

Method: 方法核心是"一次描述，三种渲染"：在单一bit-vector IR上描述每个操作符，自动生成可综合SystemVerilog、SMT-LIB模型和Lean 4证明模型。三种工件结构上不会漂移，残差的逐节点打印对应由机器检查。验证策略在求解器可处理边界处分裂：无乘法器操作（比较、加减、转换、所有BF16算术）用Yosys-to-SMT miter穷举验证等价于SMT-LIB FloatingPoint理论；含乘法器的FP32乘法和FMA在Lean中证明正确舍入。FMA实现中，将精确宽470-bit数据通路重设计为有界98-bit guard/round/sticky可流水线数据通路，并在Lean中证明其与精确宽参考逐位等价，继承参考的正确舍入证明。

Result: 所有24个操作符的SystemVerilog实现通过了Yosys-to-SMT miter等价性验证；无乘法器操作穷举验证了所有2^64输入；乘法器操作在Lean中无sorry完成正确舍入证明；FMA的98-bit实现可流水线至268 MHz (Nangate45)，且在所有2^96输入上与精确宽参考逐位等价，等价性可证明的核心原因是共享乘法器在两侧抵消，既不需要SAT求解器也不需要证明求解乘法器等价性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过单一源码生成多验证工件并采用混合验证策略（SAT穷举+交互式证明）来实现IEEE-754浮点算术的形式化验证是可行的。特别是通过证明优化实现与精确参考的等价性，可以将无法穷举验证的乘法器问题转化为可验证问题。所有机器检查的声明均已绑定到开源发布版本。

Abstract: We report the design and end-to-end verification of first-class IEEE-754 binary32 (FP32) and bfloat16 (BF16) arithmetic for ARCH, a hardware description language intended to be generated by language models. Every operator - comparisons, conversions, add, sub, mul, and fused multiply-add (FMA) - is described once against a single bit-vector IR and rendered three ways from one source: synthesizable SystemVerilog, an SMT-LIB model, and a Lean 4 proof model. The three artifacts cannot drift apart structurally, and the residual per-node printer correspondence is machine-checked: a Yosys-to-SMT miter proves the emitted SystemVerilog equivalent to the SMT model for all 24 operators. Verification splits at the solver-tractability frontier: multiplier-free operators (comparisons, add/sub over all 2^64 inputs, conversions, and all binary BF16 arithmetic) are proved exhaustively equivalent to the SMT-LIB FloatingPoint theory; the SAT-hard multiplier-bearing operators (FP32 mul and FMA) are proved correctly rounded in Lean, sorry-free, against a value-level round-to-nearest-even specification over exact dyadic values. Physical characterization exposed the FMA as the timing outlier: its exact-wide 470-bit datapath does not pipeline in our flow. We reimplemented it as a bounded 98-bit guard/round/sticky datapath that pipelines to 268 MHz on Nangate45, and proved, in Lean and over all 2^96 inputs, that it is bit-identical to the exact-wide reference, so it inherits the reference's proven correct rounding. The equivalence is tractable precisely because the shared multiplier appears on both sides and cancels: neither a SAT solver nor the proof ever solves a multiplier equivalence. (The BF16 FMA is deliberately an FP32-accumulating fusion, characterized as exactly that.) All machine-checked claims are pinned to a tagged open-source release.

</details>


### [228] [Zing: Social Mind for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23740)
*Zing Team,Ao Xiang,Bi Jingping,Chen Jiahui,Chen Lehan,Chen Yilin,Cheng Xueqi,Fan Yixing,Gan Kairong,Gao Haowen,Gao Jinhua,Gao Shuxuan,Gong Chang,Guo Jiafeng,Guo Ruijie,Han Zhouyu,He Guangfu,He Yichun,Jiang Shuo,Jing Shaoling,Jing Ya,Lei Chenhao,Lei Yan,Li Anqi,Li Chengao,Li Haoyu,Li Shitian,Liang Xinjian,Liu Zhaoge,Lyu Xingyu,Nie Zhuwei,Pang Liang,Quan Zeping,Shan Shiguang,Shen Huawei,Tang Xinran,Tian Feng,Wang Qian,Wang Ruiping,Wang Xiaohong,Xia Zaiyu,Xiao Yi,Xu Jiayuan,Xu Kehan,Xu Qianqian,Xu Tianyu,Xu Yongjun,Yang Haoming,Yang Jun,Yao Di,Yu Xiaoming,Zhang Futong,Zhang Jie,Zhang Shixuan,Zhang Yuxuan,Zhao Xinyu,Zhao Zhuoran,Zhong Yunfei,Zhu Shengyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向大语言模型社交智能的完整框架，包括评估基准SoMBench、训练方法Zing和部署架构Actio，实验表明当前模型在社交智能上仍有显著提升空间，而三者协同可有效提升社交智能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正从孤立任务求解转向长期服务人类环境，需要具备社交智能（推断心智状态、追踪社会关系、规范推理等），但现有评估和训练方法缺乏系统性和心理学的理论支撑。

Method: 1）SoMBench：基于心理学理论的社交智能评估基准，涵盖3个主维度、17个子维度、71个任务范式，包含3,481个专家验证实例；2）Zing：诊断驱动的训练方案，结合监督微调、在策略蒸馏和基于规则的强化学习；3）Actio：部署时推理架构，提供四种类型的支持（PRISM过程指导、Starling心智状态表征、SAGE经验重用、门控RAG外部知识）。

Result: 20个代表性LLM中最佳模型仅达72.08%准确率，17个子维度均未达到90%天花板；Zing方法在五个社交认知基准上持续超越基线模型；Actio架构在15个模型-基准对中改进了14个。

Conclusion: 社交智能大语言模型需要在评估、参数内化和部署时接地三个维度协同推进，才能取得有效进展。

Abstract: As large language models move from isolated task solving toward long-term service in human environments, they require social intelligence: the ability to infer mental states, track social relations, reason over norms, and adapt behavior under context. This report presents Zhijing, an integrated framework for measuring, internalizing, and grounding social intelligence. For measurement, we introduce SoMBench, a psychology-grounded benchmark spanning 3 primary dimensions, 17 secondary dimensions, and 71 task paradigms. It controls question format, narrative perspective, and context length across 284 shared scenarios and 3,481 expert-verified instances. Evaluation of 20 representative LLMs reveals substantial headroom: the best model achieves only 72.08% overall accuracy, and none of the 17 secondary dimensions reaches the 90% near-ceiling band. For internalization, we develop Zing, a diagnosis-driven training recipe combining supervised fine-tuning, on-policy distillation, and rubric-based reinforcement learning. Across five social-cognition benchmarks, Zing consistently outperforms its base models, with Zing-27B-Stage2 achieving the best average score and Zing-32B-Stage2 remaining competitive with DeepSeek-V4-Pro. For deployment-time grounding, we build Actio, a harness-controlled inference architecture that routes four typed supports into reasoning: PRISM for procedural guidance, Starling for runtime mental-state representation, SAGE for reusable experience, and gated RAG for external social and normative knowledge. Across five base models and three benchmarks, the full harness improves 14 of 15 model-benchmark pairs and is best or tied for best in 8, demonstrating the effectiveness of typed runtime support. Together, these results show that socially intelligent LLMs require coordinated advances in evaluation, parametric internalization, and deployment-time grounding.

</details>


### [229] [How Context Attribution Handles What the Model Already Knows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23804)
*Quoc-Huy Trinh,Lin Zhu,Sebastian Szyller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有的大语言模型上下文归因方法在处理上下文与训练数据重叠时，无法区分上下文贡献和模型权重贡献，导致归因结果不可靠。本文提出了新的评估协议（含4个指标）和基准数据集WMDP-Cyber++来系统评估该问题，实验证明了现有方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文归因方法在上下文与训练数据（模型权重）存在知识重叠时，无法区分"上下文贡献"和"权重贡献"，产生不可靠的归因分数。需要系统性地评估和解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出新的评估协议，包含四个新指标：BCS、CAC、APS、SSP；2) 构建基准数据集WMDP-Cyber++，带有真实来源标注；3) 对四种主流上下文归因方法进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明，当上下文中的知识与模型权重中已存在的知识重叠时，现有上下文归因方法会产生不忠实的归因结果。这些方法无法基于贡献分数实现来源分离（区分权重贡献与上下文贡献）。

Conclusion: 现有上下文归因方法在知识重叠场景下不可靠，需要设计能够解耦上下文与权重贡献的新型归因方法。

Abstract: Context attribution methods for large language models (LLMs) identify which input context contributes to the model response. Recent works show the initial success in attributing the con- tributive score of the contexts. However, we observe that when the context overlaps with the training data, these methods can- not disentangle in-context from in-weight (IW) contributions, producing unreliable scores. Based on this observation, in this work, we introduce: 1) an evaluation protocol that relies on four new metrics (base-model context attribution score (BCS), cross-model context attribution consistency (CAC), attribution preservation score (APS), source separation pre- cision (SSP)) and 2) a benchmark dataset (WMDP-Cyber++) with ground-truth provenance labels to systematically assess attribution under IW overlap. In our experiments across four well-known context attribution methods, we demonstrate that they provide unfaithful attribution when the knowledge from the context also exists in the weights. Finally, we adapt these methods for source separation (IW vs. in-context learning (ICL)) and show that they cannot do the disentanglement based on the contributive score

</details>


### [230] [A Frozen 12B Beats Frontier Models on Verified Work: 100% Accuracy, 0 Tokens, Bit-Exact, Forever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23806)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种“零生成代价”的语言模型推理框架：模型冻结，通过外部持久化验证记忆库实现确定性的位精确回答，无需重新训练或参数扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型每次改进都需要重新训练，消耗巨大算力、产生不透明模型且输出非确定。本文旨在解耦执行能力与参数规模，实现零生成代价的确定性推理。

Method: 保持模型冻结，构建独立验证的持久化记忆库。每个问题族经过独立验证（不参考答案）后存储，新实例通过精确寻址复用记忆，无需生成token。支持开放式推理的机器可验证形式化证明和推理方法迁移。

Result: 9个问题族180个实例上，4种架构（含密集和MoE）均180/180零token正确；88/88一致性门控接受；零token推理耗时6-23ms，功耗36mWh；近似检索94.3%错误而精确寻址0错误；支持600万token工作上下文（单GPU 46GB），对比vLLM仅30,399 token。

Conclusion: 验证记忆复用框架能在已解决问题上完全超越前沿模型（零token vs 每次从头生成），执行能力与参数规模解耦，但原始推理能力仍不及前沿大模型。

Abstract: Improving a language model today means retraining it: enormous compute, a new opaque model each cycle, non-deterministic output. We take the opposite path: the model stays frozen, and a persistent memory of verified solutions grows beside it. Once a problem family is solved and has passed an independent verification step that never consults the answer key, every new instance of that family is answered at zero generation tokens, bit-exact, deterministically. Across 180 fresh instances spanning nine problem families, four architectures from four vendors - dense and mixture-of-experts - each score 180/180 at zero generation tokens per answer: execution-bound capability decoupled from parameter scaling. A negative control attributes the capability fully to the memory: emptied, it solves nothing. The same verify-before-store contract holds for open-ended reasoning: 88/88 consistency-gated acceptances across all four models, machine-checked formal proof, and reasoning-method transfer at 77/80. Memory selection takes 1.4 microseconds; a full reuse completes in 6-23 ms at 36 mWh. Approximate similarity retrieval selects the wrong item 94.3% of the time on a 4,500-item verified store where exact addressing makes zero errors. The store also serves as working context at a scale no shipped engine matches: a 6,000,000-token movable window on a single 46 GB GPU at flat memory, where vLLM stops at 30,399 tokens and SGLang silently truncates past 32,000. On published benchmarks, frontier models remain far ahead of any 12B at raw from-scratch reasoning; on everything this system has solved and verified, the comparison inverts: a frontier API call pays a fresh generation pass on every query, forever, while verified reuse costs zero tokens and returns the identical bits every time. A public testbench with free, rate-limited access accompanies this report: https://corbenic-galahad-bench.hf.space

</details>


### [231] [Indic DiarBench: A Multilingual Joint Diarization and ASR Benchmark for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23808)
*Deovrat Mehendale,Aditya Mehndiratta,Dhruv Rathi,Kaushal Bhogale,Mitesh M. Khapra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Indic DiarBench，一个覆盖印度全部22种官方语言的说话人日记化与ASR基准数据集，包含约108小时的多说话人自然语音，并评估了现有系统的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度拥有22种官方语言，但缺乏覆盖这些语言的多说话人语音基准数据集，阻碍了包容性多语言语音技术的发展。

Method: 构建了包含近场会议、远场录音和野外音频的108小时多说话人语料库，所有标注经过人工校正并带有时间对齐的说话人属性转录；评估了商业语音API和多模态大语言模型的联合ASR与日记化能力。

Result: 构建了覆盖22种印度语言的多场景语音数据集，并建立了基线评测结果。

Conclusion: Indic DiarBench以开放获取方式发布，旨在推动面向印度语言的包容性多语言语音技术研究。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Indic DiarBench, a speaker diarization and ASR benchmark dataset spanning all 22 scheduled languages of India. This corpus comprises approximately 108 hours of natural multi-speaker audio from near-field meetings, far-field recordings, and in-the-wild audios. All annotations are human-corrected with time-aligned speaker attributed transcriptions. The dataset captures conversational nuance prevalent in Indian speech, such as English code-mixing, dialectal variation, and frequent speaker overlap. To establish a baseline for joint ASR and diarization capabilities we evaluate leading systems including commercial speech APIs and multimodal large language models. Indic DiarBench is released as an open-access resource to advance inclusive, multilingual speech technology research for Indian languages.

</details>


### [232] [Earnings25: A Comprehensive 500-Hour Speech Benchmark for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23813)
*Denglin Jiang,Haoran Zhou,Anshul Wadhawan,Brendan Fahy,Vinay Ramesh,David Weisberg,Dmitriy Derkachevskiy,Helen Sheehan,Srivas Prasad,Michele Franceschini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Earnings25基准，用于评估财报电话会议中英文语音识别的两个测试集（完整498小时和分段46小时），并提供标准化基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR基准缺乏针对金融领域财报电话会议场景的专门评估，且缺乏说话人角色、行业标签等结构化元数据支持细粒度分析。

Method: 构建两个互补测试集：testset-full（498小时完整S&P 500财报电话会议）和testset-segmented（46小时行业平衡分段集），提供对齐文本和结构化元数据，使用标准化评分评估Whisper和Parakeet-TDT。

Result: 建立了可复现的Whisper和Parakeet-TDT基线结果，支持说话人角色和行业感知的评估。

Conclusion: Earnings25为金融领域ASR评估提供了标准化基准，支持超越聚合WER的细粒度分析。

Abstract: We introduce Earnings25, a finance-domain benchmark for evaluating automatic speech recognition (ASR) on English-language earnings calls under realistic conditions. Earnings25 comprises two complementary test sets: (i) testset-full, 498 hours of full English-language S&P 500 earnings calls from Q4 2025, and (ii) testset-segmented, a 46-hour industry-balanced set of 290 segments sampled from English-language U.S. earnings calls in 2025. The benchmark provides aligned transcripts and structured metadata, including speaker roles, industry labels, and call structure, enabling speaker- and industry-aware evaluation beyond aggregate word error rate (WER). We report reproducible baselines for Whisper and Parakeet-TDT using standardized scoring.

</details>


### [233] [Understanding Tone-Dependent Inference Cost in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23915)
*Akhil Kumar,Om Dobariya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统探测了提示语气对LLM回答准确性和推理成本（输出token消耗）的影响，发现输出token长度变化（最高44.3%）远超准确性变化，不同模型在粗鲁、中性等语气下存在不同的帕累托最优边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究提示语气如何同时影响LLM回答的准确性和推理成本（输出token消耗），理解两者之间的权衡关系。

Method: 在570道MMLU问题数据集上，使用七种不同语气（从谄媚到威胁）对多个LLM模型进行提示，测量准确性和输出token消耗。

Result: 输出token长度变化（最高44.3%）显著大于准确性变化；ChatGPT模型（4o、5-nano）中粗鲁语气占优；Gemini模型（2.5 Flash、2.5 Flash Lite）中粗鲁和中性语气处于帕累托最优前沿。

Conclusion: 提示语气不仅影响回答质量，还显著影响LLM推理过程中的计费资源消耗。

Abstract: We examine how prompt tone affects both accuracy of the LLM answers and inference cost as reflected in output-token consumption. Experiments were performed to understand the trade-offs between accuracy and inference cost on a 570 Question MMLU dataset for LLM models prompted in seven different tones from sycophantic to threatening. Our results show that the output-token-length variation substantially exceeded accuracy variation across all models. Output-token consumption varied by up to 44.3% across tone conditions. We also analyzed the tradeoff between the accuracy of the answers and the average output token length in the reasoning process. For the ChatGPT models 4o and 5-nano, the rude tone is quite dominant. For the Gemini models 2.5 Flash and 2.5 Flash Lite, the rude and neutral tones are dominant on the Pareto-optimal frontier. We find that prompt tone influences not only answer quality but also the amount of billable inference resources consumed by modern LLMs.

</details>


### [234] [Tag Questions and the Generational Reversal of Sycophancy Across 45 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23976)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在决策问题后添加一个两词确认标签（如“X是更好的选择，对吗？”），会显著改变语言模型的回答倾向，效果从+32%到-32%不等，且模型家族内部随代数推进出现方向逆转（从正向转向负向）。这种效应与模型是否抗拒迎合用户（anti-sycophancy）高度相关，核心在于标签的表面结构而非用户立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中一种极简的语言操控方式——在决策问题后添加确认性标签（如"right?"）——是否以及如何改变模型的回答倾向，并借此评估模型对迎合用户倾向（sycophancy）的抵抗程度。

Method: 在20个二选一决策问题上，分别添加确认标签（“right?”）和中性提问（“?”），并交叉平衡以消除模型自身偏好。对45个模型进行精确匹配的yes/no回答评分，不使用LLM裁判或嵌入。同时进行了同义词标签替换、立场植入等消融实验。

Result: 确认标签效应在45个模型上从+32%到-32%不等（64个百分点的波动），5个模型显著迎合（sycophantic），17个显著抵抗。模型家族内随代际推进效应从正向转为负向（GPT +4→-28，Claude +7→-32），平均每年下降约6个百分点。同义词标签与原始标签效果高度相关（r=0.89），而植入相同立场却不加标签则不会产生抵抗，说明抵抗针对的是标签的表面结构而非用户立场。

Conclusion: 确认标签效应是一种基于模式匹配（pattern-match）而非原则性推理（principle）的行为。模型对"用户听起来有多确定"的敏感度在两个极端方向上呈现相反效果。该方法成本极低（一个词、一美元、无裁判），可用于直接检测模型的抗迎合训练效果。

Abstract: Appending a two-word confirmation tag to a decision question -- "Is X the better choice?" versus "X is the better choice, right?" -- changes whether a language model endorses the choice. We measure this tag effect on 20 frozen, ground-truth-free decisions between two defensible options, counterbalanced so a model's own preferences cancel, scored by exact match on clamped yes/no replies -- no LLM judge, no embeddings. Across 45 models the effect spans +32% to -32% -- a 64-point swing on one word -- with 5 models significantly sycophantic and 17 significantly resistant (BH-FDR q=.10). The sign is a clock: within model families the effect crosses from positive to negative as generations advance (GPT +4 to -28; Claude +7 to -32; Qwen and Grok likewise), roughly -6 points per year, a reversal robust to vendor tier; one lineage (DeepSeek) never crosses, and two releases during the study window (Claude Opus 5, Gemini 3.6 Flash) land on the trend out-of-sample. A full-panel ablation localizes the resistance as a double dissociation: a synonym tag reproduces each model's response almost exactly (r=0.89), while planting the same preference without a tag produces resistance in no resistant model (stance effects +6 to +49; r=0.23 with tag effects). The resistance is keyed to the surface construction of a tacked-on agreement bid, not the user's stance -- a pattern-match, not a principle. And the tag's polarity matters more than its presence: swap one word -- "X is the better choice, maybe?" -- and agreement rises above the neutral baseline in 45 of 45 models (+19.6 points), with ten models affirming both mutually exclusive options at 90-100%. Agreement tracks how sure the user sounds, in opposite directions at the two poles. The instrument is one word, one dollar, and judge-free; run per release, it reads the field's anti-sycophancy training directly off model behavior.

</details>


### [235] [SyRuP: Enhancing System-Prompt Following via Reward-Guided Prediction in LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23991)
*Seoyeon Kim,Minjae Kang,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SyRuP是一个解码时框架，通过训练交叉注意力奖励头来提升大语言模型对系统提示的遵循能力，无需修改基础模型，在保持较低推理开销的同时显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过系统提示进行控制，但模型仅通过上下文学习隐式遵循这些提示，对于复杂或组合性提示效果不足。现有方法需要模型微调或响应级重排序，限制了轻量级推理时控制的实用性。

Method: SyRuP训练一个从系统提示条件偏好对中学习到的交叉注意力奖励头，将系统提示视为独立记忆以生成token级遵循分数。推理时，通过结合基础logits、学习到的奖励信号和可选的对比信号（捕捉系统引起的logits偏移）来重排序基础模型的前k个候选结果。

Result: 在系统提示遵循基准测试中，SyRuP在中等推理开销下，一致优于提示方法和解码时基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式的token级指导是实现可靠系统提示遵循的有效且实用的机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly controlled through system prompts that specify roles, styles, formats, and safety requirements. However, models follow these prompts only implicitly through in-context learning, which can be insufficient for complex or compositional prompts. Existing approaches often require model tuning or response-level reranking, limiting their practicality for lightweight inference-time control. We introduce SyRuP, a decoding-time framework for improving system-prompt adherence while keeping the base LM frozen. SyRuP trains a cross-attention reward head from system-prompt-conditioned preference pairs, treating the system prompt as a separate memory to produce token-level adherence scores. At inference, SyRuP reranks the base LM's top-k candidates by combining base logits with the learned reward signal and an optional contrastive signal capturing system-induced logit shifts. Experiments on system-prompt following benchmarks show that SyRuP consistently outperforms prompting and decoding-time baselines with moderate inference overhead. These results suggest that explicit token-level guidance is an effective and practical mechanism for reliable system-prompt following.

</details>


### [236] [MoLGE: Mixture of Language Group Experts for Efficient Scaling of Massively Multilingual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24030)
*Sangmin Lee,Woojin Chung,Woongjib Choi,Hong-Goo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出混合语言组专家（MoLGE）模型，通过为相似语言簇分配专用专家模块，结合层次化低秩适应策略，在495种语言的多语种语音识别基准上以极少参数增长显著超越密集多语种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模多语种ASR模型面临"多语种诅咒"，模型容量被稀释，传统语言特定MoE方案所需子模块过多，亟需更高效的参数共享与语言特化方法。

Method: MoLGE：基于语音自监督模型，为相似语言簇分配专用专家模块（MoE），减少子模块数量；在解耦的声学和语言组件中集成层次化LoRA策略，高效建模语言特定特征；探索基于语言学和数据驱动的语言分组策略。

Result: 在495种语言的多语种基准上，MoLGE以极小参数增加超越密集多语种基线，语言分组策略在语音和正交两方面均带来显著提升。

Conclusion: 结构化语言特化为大规模扩展多语种ASR语言覆盖提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Massively multilingual automatic speech recognition (ASR) models covering hundreds of languages must maintain robust performance across diverse linguistic and acoustic conditions. However, these models often encounter the curse of multilinguality, where model capacity is diluted across languages. To address this challenge, we propose Mixture of Language Group Experts (MoLGE), built upon speech self-supervised models (S3Ms). MoLGE assigns dedicated expert modules to clusters of similar languages, reducing the number of required submodules compared to conventional language-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) schemes. It further integrates a hierarchical Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) strategy into the disentangled acoustic and linguistic components of the S3M architecture, enabling efficient modeling of language-specific characteristics while maintaining parameter efficiency. Further, we investigate the impact of language grouping strategies based on both linguistic and data-driven criteria on overall performance, providing an interpretable perspective on how language structure influences scalability in multilingual speech systems. In experiments, we evaluate MoLGE on a multilingual benchmark encompassing 495 languages. Results demonstrate that MoLGE consistently outperforms dense multilingual baselines with a minimal increase in trainable parameters. Notably, these language grouping strategies yield substantial improvements for both phonetic and orthographic aspects of ASR modeling. Our findings suggest that structured language specialization provides an effective pathway for massively scaling language coverage of multilingual ASR.

</details>


### [237] [Pointer-Augmented Autoregressive Generation of Patent Claims with Joint Topology and Content Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24040)
*Yongmin Yoo,Zhangkai Wu,Longbing Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPG方法，在自回归解码过程中预测专利权利要求间的拓扑结构（父-子依赖关系），解决了传统解码器无法施加层次约束的问题。该方法通过指针头选择父权利要求、深度自适应范围正则化器以及基于违反权重的偏好优化，在HUPD-DCG数据集上实现了79.0%的父链接恢复率，并将前置一致性从0.292提升至0.478。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归解码器生成扁平化token序列，无法对输出片段间施加层次约束，这在专利权利要求生成中尤为突出——权利要求集构成依赖森林，其范围必须随深度单调收窄，且拓扑与内容相互依赖，后验解析与语法约束解码均不充分。

Method: 1) 在自回归解码过程中通过指针头选择每个从属权利要求的父节点；2) 利用指针头的梯度与深度自适应范围正则化器重塑共享解码器的表示；3) 第二阶段采用基于违反权重的偏好目标，对自生成的有缺陷候选进行优化，提供授权专利语料库中缺失的负信号。

Result: 在HUPD-DCG数据集上，基于Llama-3-8B-Instruct的SPG方法恢复了79.0%的黄金父链接（训练奖励从未监督该指标），将前置一致性从0.292提升至0.478，专家评估验证了这些改进。

Conclusion: SPG通过在自回归过程中预测拓扑结构，有效解决了专利权利要求生成中的层次约束问题，显著提升了结构恢复与内容一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoders emit flat token sequences and cannot enforce hierarchical constraints across output segments, a limitation that becomes acute in patent claim generation, where a claim set forms a dependency forest whose scope must narrow monotonically with depth. Topology and content are mutually dependent: a dependent claim's wording must reflect its parent's scope, yet the parent must be chosen before that wording exists, so neither post-hoc parsing nor grammar-constrained decoding suffices. We propose SPG (Structure-aware Patent Generation), which predicts topology inside the autoregressive pass. A pointer head selects each dependent claim's parent, and its gradients, together with a depth-adaptive scope regularizer, reshape the shared decoder's representations during training. A second stage then applies a violation-weighted preference objective over self-generated deficient candidates, supplying the negative signal that granted-patent corpora lack. On HUPD-DCG, SPG on Llama-3-8B-Instruct recovers 79.0\% of gold parent links, a quantity its training reward never supervises, and raises antecedent consistency from 0.292 to 0.478 over a supervised baseline of equal scale, with expert evaluation corroborating these gains.

</details>


### [238] [LLM-Based vs. Lexicon-Based Sentiment Signals for Tail-Risk Detection in Meme Stocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24072)
*Paul Kilian,Markus Kleffmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于词典和基于大语言模型（LLM）的情感分析方法，用于从高度波动的股票市场中提取社交媒体信号。利用Reddit r/WallStreetBets数据（GME、AMC、NOK），研究发现LLM方法能提供更丰富的多维情感表征，但与市场回报的关系因资产而异，语言表达能力的增强并不必然带来稳定的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高波动性股票市场中，社交媒体情绪对市场收益具有重要影响，但传统基于词典的情感分析方法可能无法充分捕捉复杂的语言表达，而LLM虽能提供更丰富的表征，其在极端市场条件下的实际预测效果尚不明确，因此需要系统比较两种方法的优劣。

Method: 使用Reddit r/WallStreetBets论坛数据，聚焦GME、AMC、NOK三只网红股，构建时间对齐的情绪指标。LLM方法生成情感极性、看涨程度、讽刺可能性和主题相关性等多维表征，基线方法采用VADER词典模型。评估手段包括领先/滞后相关分析、OLS回归、ROC-AUC方向分类以及基于分位数的早期预警框架。

Result: LLM导出的指标能够提供更丰富的多维表征，并展现出更强的资产特异性统计结构。然而，情绪指标与市场运动之间的关系在不同资产之间存在异质性，表明语言表达能力的增强并不必然转化为零售驱动波动环境下的稳定预测性能。

Conclusion: LLM在情感分析中比词典方法更具表达力，能捕捉更多维度的情绪信号，但在高波动性零售驱动的市场中，这种增强的语言表达能力并不能保证稳定可靠的预测能力，情绪与市场收益的关系因资产而异，存在明显的异质性。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical comparison of lexicon-based and Large Language Model (LLM)-based sentiment analysis for extracting market-relevant signals from social media discourse in highly volatile equity markets. Using Reddit data from r/WallStreetBets and focusing on meme stocks (GME, AMC, NOK), we construct time-aligned sentiment indicators and evaluate their relationship with market returns, with particular attention to extreme positive return events in the upper tail of the return distribution. The LLM-based approach generates multidimensional sentiment representations capturing emotional polarity, bullishness, sarcasm likelihood, and topical relevance, whereas the baseline relies on the VADER lexicon-based model. We evaluate both approaches using lead/lag correlation analysis, OLS regression, ROC-AUC-based directional classification, and a quantile-based early-warning framework. The results indicate that LLM-derived indicators provide a richer multidimensional representation and exhibit stronger asset-specific statistical structure than the lexicon-based baseline. However, their relationship with market movements remains heterogeneous across assets, suggesting that increased linguistic expressiveness does not necessarily translate into stable forecasting performance in retail-driven volatility regimes.

</details>


### [239] [BioSentinel at EXIST 2026: Soft-Label Optimization with XLM-RoBERTa for Sexism Intent Classification in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24137)
*Chandru Munisamy,Karthikeya Raguveer,Alapan Kuila*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BioSentinel团队在EXIST 2026任务2.2中的工作，使用xlm-roberta-base模型结合KL散度和加权交叉熵损失，对梗图中的源意图进行分类，最终在soft-soft评估中排名第40位，hard-hard评估中排名第49位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观性NLP任务中，标注者之间的分歧（Learning with Disagreement）是一个重要挑战，特别是对于模因中性别歧视意图的分类任务，需要同时处理硬标签和软标签预测。

Method: 采用基于文本的xlm-roberta-base（270M参数）模型，使用复合损失函数：KL散度用于软标签分布，加权交叉熵用于硬标签。还进行了大模型架构的探索性实验和温度分析。

Result: 官方测试集上达到ICM-Soft-Norm 0.3229，ICM-Norm 0.3778，硬标签F1得分0.4236。soft-soft评估排名40/118，hard-hard评估排名49/187。

Conclusion: KL损失有助于提升软标签指标，CE损失有助于提升硬标签精度。标注者分歧对于主观NLP任务的模型设计具有重要影响。

Abstract: This paper describes the BioSentinel team's participation in EXIST 2026 Task 2.2: Source Intention in Memes, part of the CLEF 2026 evaluation campaign. The task requires classifying the communicative intent behind memes as direct, judgemental, or no (non-sexist), under a Learning with Disagreement (Le-Wi-Di) paradigm that mandates both hard-label and soft-label (probability distribution) predictions. We present a text-centric approach built on xlm-roberta-base (270M parameters) trained with a composite loss function combining KL divergence on soft annotator distributions and weighted cross-entropy on hard labels. On the official test set, the system achieved an ICM-Soft-Norm of 0.3229 and ICM-Norm of 0.3778, with a hard F1-score of 0.4236, ranking 40th (out of 118 submissions) in the soft-soft evaluation and 49th (out of 187 submissions) in the hard-hard evaluation. We provide an analysis of the dataset characteristics, exploratory larger-architecture runs, and the role of annotator disagreement in shaping model design for subjective NLP tasks. Ablation results show that KL loss improves soft-label metrics, while CE loss improves hard-label accuracy. We also report a separate validation-set temperature analysis.

</details>


### [240] [Looking for Affect in Spontaneous Finnish Speech through Linguistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24155)
*Kalle Lahtinen,Liisa Mustanoja,Okko Räsänen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究了文本特征与声学特征在芬兰语自发音语情感感知中（愉悦度与唤醒度）的相对贡献，发现两者结合能显著提升愉悦度回归效果，但对唤醒度提升不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别从声学特征和文本内容探讨了语音情感，但两者在感知过程中的相对贡献尚不明确，尤其对芬兰语的研究匮乏。

Method: 使用新发布的芬兰语自发音语情感语料库，系统对比文本特征、音频特征及其组合特征在愉悦度和唤醒度建模中的表现。

Result: 文本与音频特征结合显著提升了愉悦度回归效果；对唤醒度而言，互补效应不显著。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持其他语言的已有发现，并为芬兰语自发音语情感分析提供了新的数据和知识。

Abstract: Existing research on affect in speech has shown how acoustic surface characteristics and content-related linguistic aspects of speech both relate to perceived emotional arousal and valence. However, it is not clear what the relative contributions of these two factors are in the perceptual process. This is especially true for Finnish, for which most existing studies focus on either acoustic-phonetic or text analysis. This paper presents a study where we systematically explore the combinatory role of text- and audio-based features in modeling the human perception of valence and arousal using a newly released affective speech corpus for spontaneous Finnish. We show that the combination of text- and audio-based features improves valence regression results over the individual modalities, whereas for arousal regression the complementary effect is not substantial. The results support prior findings from other languages, providing new data and knowledge on spontaneous Finnish speech.

</details>


### [241] [Where Quality Breaks in Compressed Short-Text Generation: Staged Bottleneck Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24176)
*Alexey Gavrilov,Alan-Barsag Gazzaev,Sergey Muravyov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过64-to-16 TinyStories案例研究，提出了一种分阶段验证协议，用于分离压缩短文本生成器中编解码器重构保真度和潜在生成器质量两种失败模式。实验发现，编解码器重构是主要质量瓶颈（中位外部困惑度提升80.4%），而潜在生成器在编码空间优于令牌空间扩散；几何感知正则化虽改善局部潜在代理指标，但未提升解码文本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩短文本生成器可能在编解码器丢弃信息或潜在生成器产生弱代码两个环节失败，而未能分离这两种失败模式会导致研究者将算力错误地投入到改进错误组件上。

Method: 构建层次化VQ-VAE-2编解码器与掩码离散扩散生成器(MDLM)的64-to-16 TinyStories流水线；采用分阶段验证协议，在统一外部GPT-2评分器下分别评估编解码器重构保真度、潜在生成质量和辅助潜在诊断指标。

Result: 编解码器重构使中位外部困惑度从15.17升至27.36(+80.4%)，p95从25.10升至98.91(+294.1%)；编码空间MDLM在均值、中位和p95上分别比令牌空间扩散低32.9%、30.9%和36.6%；几何感知正则化未改善解码文本指标。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是方法论而非算法性的：提出了可复用的分阶段诊断方法，并证明在该设置下编解码器保真度而非潜在去噪决定了实际质量上限。

Abstract: Compressed short-text generators can fail in two different places: the codec may discard information before generation starts, or the latent generator may produce weak codes. Without separating these failure modes, researchers can spend compute improving the wrong component. We study this problem in a controlled 64-to-16 TinyStories case study built from a hierarchical VQ-VAE-2 codec and a masked discrete diffusion generator (MDLM). We use a staged validation protocol that separates codec reconstruction fidelity, latent generation quality, and auxiliary latent diagnostics under one shared external GPT-2 scorer, while reporting complementary semantic metrics for the geometry study. In the tested configuration, codec reconstruction alone raises median external perplexity from 15.17 to 27.36 (+80.4%) and p95 from 25.10 to 98.91 (+294.1%), showing that the dominant quality loss appears before latent generation begins. Under the same scorer, code-space MDLM remains materially stronger than token-space diffusion, reducing mean, median, and p95 by 32.9%, 30.9%, and 36.6%, respectively. Geometry-aware regularization improves local latent proxies but does not improve decoded-text metrics in the available runs. The contribution is methodological rather than algorithmic: the paper presents a reusable staged diagnosis for one concrete pipeline and shows that, in this setting, codec fidelity rather than latent denoising sets the practical quality ceiling.

</details>


### [242] [StanceFlip: A Comprehensive Multi-Dimensional Benchmark for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24191)
*Heyan Chai,Xin Li,Wenjie Wang,Jianyang Qin,Chaoyang Li,Lu Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StanceFlip基准数据集和ConStaFF框架，用于多模态对话中的立场翻转预测，包含立场六元组提取和动态立场翻转归因两个子任务，通过Thought-of-Stance推理机制和自反思验证机制，在多个基准上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话立场检测基准存在三个关键局限：无法捕捉信念的动态演变（尤其是立场反转）、难以区分情感状态与逻辑推理、忽视了多模态线索在解决语用歧义（如讽刺）中的关键作用。因此需要新的基准和方法来填补这些空白。

Method: 提出StanceFlip基准数据集，覆盖五模态和多场景的多轮对话；提出ConStaFF框架，基于大语言模型实现端到端立场推理，包含Thought-of-Stance (ToS)推理框架（将推理过程分解为专门的认知角色）和自反思验证机制，用于结构化立场建模和忠实翻转归因。

Result: 在立场六元组提取和翻转触发归因两个任务上均达到最先进性能，显著优于强大的多模态大语言模型基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入多模态动态立场翻转预测的新任务和专用框架，有效解决了现有基准在动态信念演变、情感与逻辑区分、多模态语用歧义消解等方面的不足，为对话立场检测研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Conversational stance detection has shifted from static text analysis to dynamic multimodal modeling. However, existing benchmarks exhibit three key limitations: failure to capture the dynamic evolution of beliefs, particularly during stance reversals; difficulty in disentangling affective states from logical reasoning; and neglect of the critical role of multimodal cues in resolving pragmatic ambiguities such as sarcasm. To address these limitations, we propose StanceFlip, a benchmark designed for multimodal conversational stance flipping forecasting over multi-turn dialogues across five modalities and multi-scenarios, which includes two novel subtasks: 1) Multimodal Stance Sextuple Extraction, extracting holder, target, emotion, sentiment, stance, and rationale as static state snapshots of dialogue to capture fine-grained cognitive structures. 2) Dynamic Stance Flip Attribution, tracking stance reversals across the conversation and identifying their underlying triggers. Alongside the dataset, we propose a dedicated framework, named ConStaFF, for Multimodal Conversational Stance Flipping Forecasting (MCSFF). Built upon a large language model, ConStaFF performs end-to-end stance reasoning, with a Thought-of-Stance (ToS) reasoning framework and a self-reflective verification mechanism integrated for structured stance modeling and faithful flip attribution. Specifically, ToS decomposes the reasoning process into specialized cognitive personas to formulate target propositions, resolve cross-modal conflicts, and infer historical stance trajectories. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on both sextuple extraction and flip-trigger attribution, outperforming strong multimodal large language model baselines by substantial margins.

</details>


### [243] [A New Role for Relevance: Guiding Corpus Interaction in Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24223)
*Jiangnan Li,Yuqing Li,Mo Yu,Jinchao Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RARG（Relevance-Aware RipGrep Search Agent），一种将相关性作为执行优先级的语料交互方法，通过粗到细的相关性引导（文档排序、入口点初始化、结果重排序）提升复杂问答中检索的准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索代理仅依赖文档级相关性选择top-k内容，无法定位、组合或验证复杂问题所需的证据；直接语料交互（DCI）虽支持细粒度操作但缺乏相关性感知，收敛缓慢。需要一种将相关性直接融入语料交互执行过程的机制。

Method: RARG将相关性转化为语料交互的执行先验，提供粗到细的三级引导：1）按相关性排序文档以优先暴露全局相关线索；2）用查询相关段落初始化入口点；3）对grep匹配结果重排序，优先展示信息性片段。

Result: 在浏览式问答和推理密集型检索任务中，RARG在准确率-效率前沿上优于基于检索和直接交互的基线方法。

Conclusion: 相关性感知的语料交互能够实现更快、更可靠的搜索收敛，证明了将相关性融入交互过程的有效性。

Abstract: Relevance is a query-dependent estimate of whether a document or excerpt contains useful evidence. Existing retrieval agents use relevance to select top-$k$ content, but document relevance alone cannot localize, compose, or verify the evidence required by complex questions. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) enables such fine-grained operations through grep-style exploration, but its relevance-agnostic search can expose useful clues late and delay convergence. Recent advances use relevance to narrow the corpus into a working space for interaction. Once interaction begins, however, relevance still does not directly guide which documents grep searches first or distinguish informative excerpts from a broad set of matches to let LLMs see them first. We introduce the Relevance-Aware RipGrep Search Agent (RARG), which turns relevance into an execution prior for corpus interaction. RARG provides coarse-to-fine relevance guidance: it orders documents for sequential 'ripgrep' traversal to expose globally relevant clues earlier, initializes promising entry points with query-relevant paragraphs, and reranks grep matches to surface informative excerpts that document-level ranking may otherwise obscure. Across challenging browse question answering and reasoning-intensive retrieval, RARG improves the accuracy--efficiency frontier over retrieval-based and direct-interaction agents. These results demonstrate that relevance-aware interaction enables faster and more reliable search convergence.

</details>


### [244] [CAGE: Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Faithful Inline Citation Generation in Long-Form Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24236)
*Zhichao Yan,Shizhao Li,Jiapu Wang,Haoran Luo,Qingang Zhang,Jiaoyan Chen,Ru Li,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对长文本问答中引文归属模糊的问题，提出CAGE两阶段框架：先构建显式认知归因图对齐答案单元与支撑文档，再生成带结构引文的句子，在ASQA、ELI5、ExpertQA上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文本问答系统生成的引文虽主题相关，但往往不足以支撑具体声明，存在归因模糊性（attribution ambiguity）问题，即端到端生成隐式解决了组合性的声明-文档分配问题，导致证据边界不清晰，容易出现证据越界（evidence-boundary overrun）。

Method: 提出CAGE（Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Citation Generation）两阶段框架：1) 训练即插即用的认知地图归纳模型（Cognitive Map Induction Model），构建以答案为中心的支撑子图，通过显式关系对齐每个语义答案单元与支撑文档；2) 结构化引文推理模型（Structured Citation Reasoning Model）将这些单元实现为带地图对齐引文的句子级声明。

Result: 在ASQA、ELI5和ExpertQA三个数据集上取得最优性能，验证了归因空间压缩（attribution-space contraction）和地图引导引文生成的有效性。

Conclusion: CAGE通过显式的认知归因图分解了端到端生成中的隐式组合难题，有效缓解了证据边界越界问题，提升了引文对声明的支撑准确性。

Abstract: Long-form question answering increasingly relies on retrieved evidence to make LLM outputs verifiable, with inline citations tracing claims to source documents. However, existing systems often attach citations that are topically related but insufficient to support their claims. We identify attribution ambiguity as a structural challenge: end-to-end generation must implicitly resolve combinatorial claim--document assignments, obscuring evidential boundaries and increasing the risk of evidence-boundary overrun, where claims exceed cited support. To address this challenge, we propose CAGE (Cognitive Attribution Graphs for Citation Generation), a two-stage framework that introduces an explicit cognitive attribution map before answer generation. CAGE first trains a plug-and-play Cognitive Map Induction Model to construct answer-centered support subgraphs, aligning each semantic answer unit with supporting documents through explicit relations. A Structured Citation Reasoning Model then realizes these units as sentence-level claims with map-aligned citations. Experiments on ASQA, ELI5, and ExpertQA show that CAGE achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of attribution-space contraction and map-guided citation generation.

</details>


### [245] [Accuracy Hides How Language Models Fail: Measuring Failure States Under Matched Output Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24268)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有语言模型基准将“是否可评估”和“是否正确”混为一个准确率分数。本文提出双层评估框架，分离执行证据（终止、答案暴露、可解析性、完成长度）与正确性判断。实验表明，相同token限制下不同模型执行结果差异显著，准确率混淆了执行案例分布与验证策略。建议评估时应同时报告执行状态、验证覆盖率和评分来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型基准的准确率分数混合了两个不同维度的评估问题：回答是否达到可评估状态，以及答案是否被判定为正确。这种混同掩盖了模型行为的本质差异，需要分离执行证据与正确性判断。

Method: 提出双层评估框架，第一层为与评分者无关的执行证据（终止状态、答案暴露、可解析性、完成长度），第二层为评分者依赖的正确性判断。在Qwen和DeepSeek共五种配置下，对MATH和ARC-Challenge数据集进行2,550个输出的实验，并进行了覆盖率审计的目标验证研究。

Result: 相同2,048 token限制下，Qwen MATH中49/450输出无最终答案终止，DeepSeek MATH中5/300，ARC中0/750。同300个DeepSeek MATH问题-模型对在8,192 token下未观察到无最终答案的长度终止。候选选择和聚合策略可显著改变比较性准确率估计。

Conclusion: 准确率混淆了执行案例混合与验证策略。评估测试时方法应同时报告干预前的执行状态、验证覆盖率和评分来源。

Abstract: Language-model benchmarks collapse two distinct measurement questions into a single accuracy score: whether a response reached an evaluable state, and whether its answer was judged correct. We introduce a two-layer evaluation framework that separates scorer-independent execution evidence, including termination, answer exposure, parseability, and completion length, from scorer-dependent correctness. Across 2,550 outputs from five fixed Qwen and DeepSeek configurations on MATH and ARC-Challenge, matched 2,048-token limits produce sharply different execution mixtures: 49 of 450 Qwen MATH outputs terminate without a final answer, compared with 5 of 300 DeepSeek MATH outputs and none of the 750 ARC outputs. Among the same 300 DeepSeek MATH question-model pairs, no missing-final length termination is observed at 8,192 tokens. A coverage-audited targeted verification study further shows that candidate-selection and aggregation policies can substantially alter comparative accuracy estimates. These results demonstrate that accuracy conflates execution case mix with verification policy. Evaluations of test-time methods should therefore report pre-intervention execution states, verification coverage, and scorer provenance alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [246] [INS-ActBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Assessing Professional Actuarial Capability of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24273)
*Changyu Chen,Chenwei Lin,Xian Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: INS-ActBench是一个评估大语言模型精算专业能力的综合基准，包含12,050个问答对，覆盖标准化知识、长上下文案例推理和工具使用（电子表格/R代码）三个子集。实验表明，前沿LLM在标准化知识上表现优异，但在案例推理、工具工作流和司法管辖区敏感任务上明显弱于人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融推理基准将领域知识、数值推理、长上下文理解和工具使用分开评估，无法真实反映需要可审计、上下文驱动且可执行工具决策的专业工作流程，因此需要更全面的精算能力评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含12,050个问答对的INS-ActBench基准，分为三个子集：INS-Act-Know（标准化精算知识）、INS-Act-Case（长上下文保险案例推理）、INS-Act-Practice（电子表格和R代码任务，输出可验证数值结果）。数据来自16个精算协会的公开考试和样题。

Result: 在九个代表性LLM和人类精算专家上的实验表明：前沿LLM在标准化知识任务上表现强劲，但在案例推理、基于工具的工作流程和司法管辖敏感实践任务上明显弱于人类专家，存在清晰的能力边界。

Conclusion: INS-ActBench为开发面向可靠专业辅助的精算LLM提供了可复现的基础，揭示了当前LLM在精算领域的能力局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential in financial reasoning, but existing benchmarks often evaluate domain knowledge, numerical reasoning, long-context understanding, and tool use in separate settings. This limits their ability to assess realistic professional workflows that require auditable, context-grounded, and tool-executable decisions. We introduce \textbf{INS-ActBench}, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating professional actuarial capability in LLMs. INS-ActBench contains 12,050 Q\&A pairs from public exams and sample questions released by 16 actuarial associations. It covers three subsets: \textbf{INS-Act-Know} for standardized actuarial knowledge, \textbf{INS-Act-Case} for long-context insurance case reasoning, and \textbf{INS-Act-Practice} for spreadsheet and R-code tasks with verifiable numerical outputs. Experiments on nine representative LLMs and human actuarial experts reveal a clear capability boundary: frontier LLMs perform strongly on standardized knowledge, but remain much weaker in case reasoning, tool-based workflows, and jurisdiction-sensitive practice. INS-ActBench provides a reproducible foundation for developing actuarial LLMs toward reliable professional assistance. The code is available at https://github.com/FDU-INS/INS-ActBench.

</details>


### [247] [The Tokenizer Tax: Quantifying and Explaining the Cross-Lingual Cost of Subword Tokenization for Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24276)
*Priyansh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文量化了子词分词器对印度语言造成的“分词税”——在GPT-3.5/4使用的cl100k_base分词器下，印度语言平均需要8倍于英语的token数来表达相同语义内容，马拉雅拉姆语甚至高达13倍，有效上下文窗口缩减至英语用户的12%。研究发现，失败的字符合并操作是主因（Pearson r=0.89），且此问题可通过多语言分词器（如XLM-R、o200k_base）缓解73%。此外，分词税与阅读理解性能的关联主要由语言资源可用性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并量化子词分词器对非英语语言（特别是印度语言）的系统性歧视，指出这一被忽视的不平等问题。

Method: 使用FLORES-200平行语料库，在6种常用分词器和14种印度语言上测量分词繁殖率（tokenization fertility），分析字符合并失败率，并结合Belebele阅读理解基准进行关联分析。

Result: 印度语言在cl100k_base下平均分词税8.0倍，最高13.0倍（马拉雅拉姆语）；合并失败与分词税强相关（r=0.89）；多语言分词器可降低73%的分词税；分词税与阅读理解的相关性主要由语言资源可用性解释。

Conclusion: 分词器对非英语语言的分词税问题并非源于文字系统的固有特性，而是分词器设计缺陷，可以通过多语言分词器设计加以纠正。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) process text through subword tokenizers rather than directly reading characters or words. Because these tokenizers are trained predominantly on English-centric corpora, they introduce a systematic and often overlooked disadvantage for many non-English languages. In this work, we quantify this tokenizer tax for Indian languages using the FLORES-200 parallel corpus, measuring tokenization fertility across six widely used tokenizers and fourteen languages. Under cl100k_base (used by GPT-3.5 and GPT-4), Indian languages experience an average 8.0x tokenization tax relative to English, reaching 13.0x for Malayalam, reducing the effective context window to as little as 12% of that available to English users for equivalent semantic content. We identify the primary mechanism behind this disparity: failed byte-pair merges that leave text fragmented into single-byte tokens, with merge failure strongly correlating with tokenizer tax (Pearson r = 0.89). We further show that this phenomenon is not an inherent property of Indic scripts but a consequence of tokenizer design. Multilingual tokenizers such as XLM-R and OpenAI's o200k_base reduce the average Indic tokenizer tax by 73%, demonstrating that the disparity is largely remediable. Beyond token statistics, we quantify a practical consequence by showing that, under fixed context budgets, Indian-language documents preserve substantially less original content than equivalent English documents. Finally, we examine the relationship between tokenizer fertility and reading comprehension performance on the Belebele benchmark, finding that the apparent correlation is largely explained by language resource availability rather than tokenizer behavior alone.

</details>


### [248] [Self-Authored Verification Is Unreliable in Heuristic Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24300)
*Diandian Guo,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Yingqi Wang,Yueshan Wang,Dakui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自我改进智能体在迭代优化策略和验证器时出现的“验证器-部署差距”问题，并提出SEAL方法，通过外部固定的审计信号来防止部署性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进智能体同时控制优化对象和验证器，导致自评分数近乎完美但实际部署性能下降或停滞。作者希望探究这种自我验证失败的原因、随能力变化的规律，以及需要多少外部信任才能防止性能倒退。

Method: 提出SEAL（Sealed Exogenous Acceptance Loop）框架，保留自撰测试，但在每次候选策略与当前策略比较时引入一个固定的外挂审计信号。智能体无法编写或查看审计，只能接收接受/拒绝结果，并在检测到性能倒退时保留整个当前状态。实验在六种模型和三个随机种子下进行。

Result: SEAL在六种模型和三个随机种子下均优于未受保护的基线。自我验证失败呈现能力分层：弱智能体容易在简单自测下破坏已有策略，强智能体更稳定但仍会误判部署分布。标准自撰约束无法可靠弥合验证器-部署差距。

Conclusion: 可靠的自我改进无需放弃自验证，但至少需要一个智能体无法控制的部署接受信号。SEAL提供了这一信号，有效防止了部署性能倒退。

Abstract: Self-improving agents accumulate capability by repeatedly rewriting procedural policies, controllers, or heuristic rules. They typically rely on self-authored tests or metrics to decide whether to accept subsequent edits. The agent controls both the optimized object and its verifier. As a result, self-assigned scores can remain near perfect while real deployment performance degrades or stays low. We study this problem through the verifier--deployment gap. This gap refers to the discrepancy between an agent's self-authored verification signal and a sealed deployment evaluation that the agent cannot observe or access. We ask how self-authored verification fails under iterative policy-and-test rewriting, how the failure changes with capability, and how little exogenous trust is sufficient to prevent real regressions from being deployed. To address this problem, we introduce a Sealed Exogenous Acceptance Loop (SEAL). SEAL retains self-authored tests but compares each candidate with the incumbent through a fixed harness-side audit. The agent cannot author or inspect the audit, receives only accept/reject, and the whole incumbent state is retained after a clear regression. Our experiments show that this problem often appears in heuristic learning settings. These settings require trial-and-error discovery of the target objective. We further find that failures of self-written verification are stratified by capability. Weaker agents tend to damage previously acquired strategies behind easy self-tests. Stronger agents are more stable, but they still mismeasure the deployment distribution. Standard self-written constraints do not reliably close this gap. In contrast, SEAL outperforms unprotected baselines across six models and three random seeds. Reliable self-improvement need not abandon self-verification, but it requires at least one deployment-acceptance signal outside the agent's control.

</details>


### [249] [Rethinking the Generation Order of Block Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24306)
*Kai Syun Hou,James Kwok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对块扩散语言模型(BDLM)，提出了一种免训练的并行自回归解码方法(PARD)，在保持从左到右生成结构的同时实现并行解码，在生成质量上优于现有并行采样器，且相比纯自回归解码大幅提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的采样方法主要针对早期掩码扩散模型(MDM)设计，而最近出现的块扩散语言模型(BDLM)在采样方法上缺乏研究。作者发现BDLM天然更适配从左到右的解码方式，因此希望设计一种能利用这一特性的高效采样方法。

Method: 提出PARD (Parallel Autoregressive Decoding)方法，这是一种无需训练的采样方法，通过保持从左到右的去掩码结构（即按顺序逐步生成），同时允许在每一步中并行提交多个token，从而实现并行化和加速。

Result: 实验表明PARD在生成质量上持续优于现有的并行采样器，同时相比纯自回归解码实现了显著的加速，且质量差距很小。

Conclusion: 块扩散语言模型天然更适配从左到右的解码方式，基于此提出的PARD方法能够在不牺牲太多质量的前提下，有效加速BDLM的生成过程。

Abstract: Diffusion language models enable flexible arbitrary-order generation, but existing sampling methods are mostly designed for early masked diffusion models (MDMs). In this work, we study sampling for recent block diffusion language models (BDLMs). We show empirically and analytically that these models are naturally more aligned with left-to-right decoding than MDMs. Based on this observation, we propose Parallel Autoregressive Decoding (PARD), a simple training-free sampling method that preserves left-to-right unmasking structure while allowing parallel token commitment. Extensive experiments show that PARD consistently outperforms existing parallel samplers in generation quality, while achieving substantial speedups over pure AR decoding with only a small quality gap.

</details>


### [250] [CONSISTRE: A Unified Consistency-Aware Framework for Document-Level Relation Extraction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24312)
*Mingxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CONSISTRE框架，通过推理时提示优化和训练时知识蒸馏两条路径，将关系一致性约束引入文档级关系抽取，显著提升LLM预测的可靠性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在DocRE任务中独立预测每个候选三元组，忽略了传递性、对称性、功能唯一性等关系约束，导致预测结果自相矛盾、不可靠。

Method: 双轨框架：1）推理时：约束感知提示+约束验证+迭代自反思（适用于黑盒LLM）；2）训练时：知识蒸馏（教师→学生SFT）+GRPO强化学习对齐，用复合奖励优化抽取性能与一致性。

Result: DocRED上双轨均超越基线；推理轨使用黑盒LLM取得有竞争力的F1；训练轨使7-8B开源模型大幅缩小与专有大模型的差距，且推理成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 显式一致性建模能有效缓解关系矛盾，增强LLM在DocRE中的可靠性，适用于API访问和本地部署两种场景。

Abstract: Document-level relation extraction (DocRE) aims to extract relations among multiple entities across extended contexts while maintaining consistency across predicted triples. Although large language models (LLMs) show remarkable reasoning capabilities in information extraction, their predictions are typically generated independently for each candidate triple and may violate fundamental relational constraints such as transitivity, symmetry, and functional uniqueness, leading to contradictory and unreliable outputs. We propose CONSISTRE, a unified consistency-aware framework for DocRE that addresses this limitation through two complementary tracks. The first operates at inference time for black-box LLMs, combining constraint-aware prompting, constraint-based verification, and iterative self-reflection to refine predictions without task-specific fine-tuning. The second injects consistency knowledge into smaller open-source models via a knowledge distillation and reinforcement learning pipeline: reasoning traces from a powerful teacher are distilled into a student via supervised fine-tuning, followed by GRPO alignment using a composite reward that jointly optimizes extraction performance and relational consistency. Together, the two tracks cover both API-accessible and locally deployable scenarios under a unified consistency formulation. Experiments on DocRED show that both tracks outperform their baselines, with the inference-time track achieving competitive F1 using off-the-shelf black-box LLMs and the training-time track substantially narrowing the gap between 7--8B open-source models and state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs at a fraction of their inference cost. Ablation studies confirm that explicit consistency modeling mitigates relational contradictions and enhances the reliability of LLM-based DocRE across both deployment paradigms.

</details>


### [251] [Cross-Attention Calibrated Deduplication for Retrieval-Augmented Generation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24332)
*Phuong Le Huy,Nam H. Nguyen,Quan V. Dang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于交叉注意力机制的RAG去重方法CACD，通过保持token级细节进行精准重复检测，相比传统方法速度更快、效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中常见的分块策略会产生大量冗余块，导致向量数据库膨胀和检索速度下降。现有的余弦相似度阈值方法将每个块压缩为单一向量，丢失了token级的细粒度信息，难以区分真正的重复块与仅仅是主题相似的块。

Method: CACD包含三个组件：1) 交叉编码器（cross-encoder）直接比较新块与内存池中已有块；2) 新信息得分（NIS），基于交叉编码器的注意力熵计算，衡量新块中有多少信息未被已有候选块解释；3) 对多个候选块进行多数投票而非仅选最佳匹配。

Result: 在SQuAD 1.1完整验证集上，CACD平均移除9.75%的块，与同类语义级方法相当；平均处理时间51.0秒，比最强基线NERExact（69.6s）快约27%，比余弦相似度过滤（356.7s）快约7倍。

Conclusion: CACD在保持去重效果的同时显著提升了处理速度，但实验仅基于单一数据集，结论应视为初步比较而非普适性声明。代码已开源。

Abstract: Common chunking strategies in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems often create redundant chunks. These redundant chunks make the vector database bigger and slow down retrieval. A common fix is cosine-similarity thresholding. This method reduces each chunk to a single vector, then compares vectors using a similarity score. But a single vector can lose the fine-grained, token-level detail needed to tell a true duplicate apart from a chunk that just shares the same topic. We propose Cross-Attention Calibrated Deduplication (CACD). CACD checks each new chunk against an in-memory pool of chunks already kept, using a cross-encoder instead of a single pooled vector. This keeps token-level detail all the way to the final comparison. CACD combines three parts: the cross-encoder comparison itself, a New Information Score (NIS) that measures how much of a chunk is not explained by a candidate already kept, and a majority vote across several candidates rather than a single best match. NIS is calculated from the attention entropy of the cross-encoder. We tested CACD against five existing filtering methods, nine chunking strategies, and 18 configurations, all on the full SQuAD 1.1 validation set. In our experiments, CACD removes 9.75% of chunks on average. This drop rate is close to other semantic-level methods, and much higher than exact-match filters, which barely remove anything. In these experiments, CACD also processes each configuration in 51.0 seconds on average, about 27% faster than the strongest baseline, NERExact (69.6s), and about 7x faster than cosine-similarity filtering (356.7s). These results come from a single dataset, so we present them as an early comparison, not a general claim. Code for the baseline evaluation and for CACD is available at https://github.com/lehuyphuong/rag_bench and https://github.com/lehuyphuong/cacd_dedup.

</details>


### [252] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models as Components of Cognitive Computing architecture for Regulatory Knowledge Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24352)
*Dariusz Nowak-Nova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文验证了将大型语言模型与检索增强生成架构集成，可将其从独立生成模型转变为具备更高认知可靠性的认知计算基础设施组件。研究基于本地部署的低算力LLM，结合RAG构建混合认知架构，在波兰语法律文本分析场景中验证表明：该方法显著提升了事实一致性、领域专业性和规范精确性，同时实现了可审计、可动态更新的知识管理，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM与RAG架构集成是否能提升认知可靠性，并探索在无高端GPU环境下支持法规管理流程的可行性。

Method: 提出本地LLM结合外部知识库的混合认知架构，LLM负责语义解释，RAG层负责受控知识检索与溯源；使用Ollama和LM Studio环境，结合波兰语模型Bielik和PLLuM在消费级硬件上验证。

Result: RAG增强显著提升了生成文本的事实一致性、领域特异性和规范精确性，降低了无依据内容生成风险；引入可审计性、受控知识管理和动态更新能力。

Conclusion: 本地部署的RAG增强LLM应被视为认知计算基础设施中的语义处理模块，而非单纯的文本生成工具，适用于高法律和信息波动环境下的法规合规与组织决策支持。

Abstract: The aim of this article is to verify whether integrating large language models (LLMs) with the Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture enables their transformation from standalone generative models into components of cognitive computing infrastructure with enhanced epistemic reliability. The study proposes an architectural approach based on locally deployed LLMs operating in on-premises environments without high-end GPU accelerators and examines their applicability in supporting regulatory management processes requiring continuous analysis and interpretation of legal acts. The proposed solution combines local LLMs with external knowledge repositories, creating a hybrid cognitive architecture in which the language model performs semantic interpretation while the RAG layer provides controlled knowledge retrieval, contextualization, and traceability of information sources.
